Any key below overrides the profile default (the values of each profile: src/lib/shared/performance.ts).",
    "profile": "small",
    "sqliteCacheMb": null, "sqliteMmapMb": null, "walAutocheckpointPages": null,
    "imageConcurrency": null, "yazConcurrency": null, "heavyConcurrency": null, "argon2MemoryMb": null, "smol": null
  },
  "classification": {
    "_comment": "Scheme taken from the library's Koha CCODE list (docs/reference/classification): single-letter genres for fiction, Dewey-like codes grouped by hundred for non-fiction, one pictogram per genre and per hundred (children inherit). Colours follow the pictogram sheet; the hundred discs mapping is [VERIFY].",
    "fictionByClass": true,
    "readingLevels": [
      { "code": "1", "label": "1ª" },
      { "code": "2", "label": "2ª" },
      { "code": "3", "label": "3ª" },
      { "code": "4", "label": "4ª" },
      { "code": "5", "label": "5ª" }
    ],
    "classPerItem": false,
    "genres": [
      { "code": "A", "label": "Romanzi e Racconti", "image": "A.svg", "color": "#e8231b" },

Env vars (`src/env.ts`): `CONFIG_DIR` (default `./local/config`, container `/config`), `DATA_DIR` (default `./local/data`, container `/data`), `DATABASE_PATH` (default `$DATA_DIR/db/biblioviva.db`), `PROTOCOL_HEADER`/`HOST_HEADER` (origin behind the proxy, ADR-007), `PORT`, `HOST`, `BODY_SIZE_LIMIT`, `LOG_LEVEL`, `TZ` (mirrors `library.timezone`).

## Appendix B — Report definition examples

A guided report (`config/reports/loans-by-class-section.json`, the pivot of class year × section with the totals, 9.20):

```json
{
  "id": "loans-by-class-section",
  "title": { "it": "Prestiti per classe e sezione", "en": "Loans by class and section" },
  "description": { "it": "Prestiti del periodo per anno di classe (righe) e sezione (colonne), con i totali…", "en": "…" },
  "category": "loans",
  "query": {
    "source": "loans",
    "filters": [{ "field": "period", "ask": true }],
    "rows": ["classYear"],
    "columns": "section",
    "measures": ["loans"],
    "totals": "both",
    "chart": "bar"
  }
}
```

An SQL report (the same idea written by hand, one row per class):

```json
{
  "id": "loans-by-class",
  "title": { "it": "Prestiti per classe", "en": "Loans by class" },
  "category": "loans",
  "restricted": false,
  "params": [
    { "name": "from", "label": { "it": "Dal", "en": "From" }, "type": "date", "required": true, "default": "schoolYearStart" },
    { "name": "to", "label": { "it": "Al", "en": "To" }, "type": "date", "required": true, "default": "today" },
    { "name": "patronType", "label": { "it": "Tipo utente", "en": "Patron type" }, "type": "patronType", "required": false }
  ],
  "sql": "SELECT l.class_label_snapshot AS class, COUNT(*) AS loans, COUNT(DISTINCT l.patron_id) AS readers FROM loan l WHERE date(l.issued_at) BETWEEN :from AND :to AND (:patronType IS NULL OR l.patron_type_snapshot = :patronType) AND l.class_label_snapshot IS NOT NULL GROUP BY l.class_label_snapshot ORDER BY MIN(l.level_position_snapshot), l.class_label_snapshot",
  "columns": [
    { "key": "class", "label": { "it": "Classe", "en": "Class" }, "type": "text" },
    { "key": "loans", "label": { "it": "Prestiti", "en": "Loans" }, "type": "int" },
    { "key": "readers", "label": { "it": "Lettori distinti", "en": "Distinct readers" }, "type": "int" }
  ],
  "chart": { "type": "bar", "x": "class", "y": "loans" },
  "rowLimit": 5000
}
```

Rules: a report has either `sql` or `query`; single `SELECT`/`WITH`; only `:name` parameters declared in `params`; executed on a read-only connection; `restricted: true` limits the report to admins; the full format is in `docs/reports-authoring.md`.

## Appendix C — Card template example (`cards/templates/default/`)

The built-in template is the folder `config/cards/templates/default/`; its files are the reference and are not repeated here.

`template.json`: name, description, the patron types it serves, bleed, fold and cut marks, cut lines and the barcode (size, text under it; the symbology follows `barcodes.patron.symbology` unless the template forces one).

`front.html`: the root `<div class="face front">` and one element per piece — logo, library name, photo (the `{{#patron.has_photo}}` section inside a grey frame), first and last name, class or "Docente", barcode, card number — each placed in mm and styled in its own `style` attribute, as the designer reads and writes them (11.16).

`back.html`: the root `<div class="face back">` with its background colour and the picture `retro.jpg` (cover, centred) in its own `style` attribute.

`style.css` (physical units; the renderer sizes each face to 85.6 × 53.98 mm, places front and back side by side and scopes these rules to this template): only the rules common to the front (position, white background, font, colour).

`assets/`: `logo.png` (school logo; placeholder in the repo), `retro.jpg` (the back illustration; the school's commissioned artwork is copied only into `CONFIG_DIR/cards/templates/default/assets/`, the repo ships a neutral placeholder pattern).

Fields (allowlist): `library.name`, `school_year`, `issued_on`, `valid_until`, `photo_url` (data URL), `barcode_svg` (raw, triple braces), `patron.first_name`, `patron.last_name`, `patron.card_number`, `patron.class_label` (as `school.classLabel` writes it, e.g. `3 A`), `patron.level_label`, `patron.level_name`, `patron.group` (the class's name, `A`), `school.group_word` (`Sezione`), `patron.patron_type`, `patron.is_student`, `patron.is_teacher`, `patron.has_photo`; lambda `{{#asset}}file{{/asset}}` (data URL of a file in `assets/`, also usable inside `style.css`). Every value is HTML-escaped except `barcode_svg`. The sheet wrapper (outside the template) adds `@page`, the unit grid (front | back, one unit per row), fold and cut marks.

## Appendix D — Z39.50 command file and UNIMARC mapping

`yaz-client` command file generated per request (`DATA_DIR/tmp/z3950-<uuid>.cmd`):

```
open tcp:opac.sbn.it:2100/nopac
charset - utf-8 utf-8
format unimarc
elements F
find @attr 1=7 9788804668237
show 1+1
show 2+1
show 3+1
show 4+1
show 5+1
quit
```

Run: `yaz-client -f <cmd> -m <out.mrc> < /dev/null` (stdin must be closed or yaz-client waits for input). Parse `Number of hits: N` from stdout; `out.mrc` contains the ISO 2709 records actually shown (extra `show` lines beyond N are harmless). Title/author queries: `find @attr 1=4 @attr 5=1 "cosmicomiche"`, `find @attr 1=1003 calvino`, `find @attr 1=1016 <words>`. Strip diacritics before sending.

UNIMARC → `BiblioDraft` mapping (`#lib/server/marc/unimarc-map.ts`):

| UNIMARC | Field | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `001` | `sourceId` | SBN BID as-is |
| `010$a` | `isbn` | first valid, normalised to ISBN-13 |
| `011$a` | `issn` | periodicals |
| `200$a` | `title` | join repeated `$a` with " ; " |
| `200$e` | `subtitle` | join with " : " |
| `200$f` (+`$g`) | `responsibility` | display string |
| `500$a` | `original_title` | only for a translation: the original language `101$c` differs from the language of the text `101$a` (Q123); SBN gives a 500 to works in their own language too, which is ignored |
| `210$a` / `214$a` | `publication_place` | prefer 214 when present |
| `210$c` / `214$c` | `publisher` | |
| `210$d` / `214$d` | `publication_year` | first 4-digit number |
| `215$a` (+`$c`, `$d`) | `physical_description` | the whole string, e.g. `120 p. : ill. ; 24 cm` |
| `606$a` (+`$x`) | `subjects[]` | join subfields with " - " |
| `676$a` | `dewey` | numeric part only, reference; local `class_code` suggested through `classification.deweyMap` |
| `700$a,$b` | `author` | `"$a, $b"`; if absent use first `701`, else `710$a`, else the special value `catalog.specialAuthors[0]` (`Anonimo`); `AA. VV.` when 200$f names three or more authors |
| `701`, `702`, `710`… | — | not stored (Q73); the statement of responsibility already carries the names |

## Appendix E — Container and deployment files

The files named below are the reference; this appendix says what each one holds.

`docker/Dockerfile` (8.1, runtime stage made distroless by ADR-020; build it with `podman build --format docker`, since the OCI format drops `HEALTHCHECK`): a build stage on `$BUILDPLATFORM` (`oven/bun:<version>-slim`, `bun install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts`, `vite build`, the CLI scripts bundled), a `debian:trixie-slim` stage that installs `yaz` and `busybox-static` and keeps only `yaz-client`, busybox and their libraries, and the runtime stage on `oven/bun:<version>-distroless` with the build, the shipped defaults, the demo portraits and the migrations, the environment defaults of 8.1, `USER bun`, the three volumes (`/config`, `/data`, `/backups`, owned by uid 1000), the health check on `/healthz` and `ENTRYPOINT ["bun", "./build"]`.

`docker/compose.yml` (works with `docker compose` and `podman compose`): the `tailscale` sidecar (pinned tag, `TS_AUTHKEY` from `.env`, `tag:biblioviva`, `TS_SERVE_CONFIG`, its health check), the `app` service in the sidecar's network namespace (no ports; the forwarded headers of ADR-007; `./config`, the `app-data` volume for `/data` and the folder `./backups` for `/backups`; `mem_limit` from the hardware profile; read-only root, no new privileges, no capabilities) and the optional `offsite` service (profile `offsite`, rclone, Q35, syncing `./backups`). `docker/compose.lan.yml` is the local-network variant of 8.8 and `docker/compose.demo.yml` the public demo of 9.17.

`docker/tailscale/serve.json`: HTTPS on 443 of the node's name proxied to the app on `127.0.0.1:3000`, Funnel off.

Tailnet prerequisites: MagicDNS and HTTPS certificates enabled in the admin console (DNS page); `tag:biblioviva` declared in `tagOwners`; an OAuth client with the `auth_keys` scope (or a reusable, pre-authorised, tagged auth key). tailscaled obtains and renews the Let's Encrypt certificate itself and caches it in the state volume. The identity headers tailscaled injects (`Tailscale-User-Login`, …) are ignored by the app (Q20).

The publish workflow is `.github/workflows/publish.yml` (8.2): one job per platform on native runners, then the multi-arch manifest on `ghcr.io`, with provenance attestations.

## Appendix F — OPAC SBN Z39.50 parameters

- Host `opac.sbn.it`, ports `2100` and `3950`; database `nopac`; anonymous.
- Record syntaxes: UNIMARC (OID 1.2.840.10003.5.1), MARC21 (5.10), SUTRS (5.101). Charset UTF-8.
- Records are returned one at a time ("i record in formato UNIMARC e MARC21 sono mostrati uno alla volta").
- Bib-1 use attributes (subset): 1 personal name, 4 title, 5 series title, 7 ISBN, 8 ISSN, 13 Dewey, 21 subject heading, 31 publication date, 53 local call number, 54 language code, 59 place of publication, 1003 author, 1016 any, 1018 publisher name, 1031 material type; relation `2=3` (equal); structure `4=1` phrase, `4=2` word, `4=4` year, `4=6` word list; truncation `5=1` right, `5=100` none; completeness `6=1`.
- Usage note from ICCU: avoid diacritics in search terms. Contact: `ic-cu.opacsbn@cultura.gov.it`. Source: https://opac.sbn.it/accesso-z39.50-a-opac-sbn and the linked "Attributi di Bib1 ed estensioni del profilo SBN" PDF.
- No SRU/SRW endpoint exists. Undocumented JSON gateway (mobile app backend, no terms published): `https://opac.sbn.it/opacmobilegw/search.json?isbn=<isbn13>` and `full.json?bid=<BID>` — see Q26.

---

## Appendix G — Label stock and label template examples

Label geometry comes from the stock, not from the template. The `a4-70x48` stock is measured from the library's current printout (`docs/reference/labels/koha-etichette.html`, generated by a Koha label plugin): body `210mm`, page `padding-top: 4mm`, `margin-left: 0`, floated cells `70mm × 96mm` each made of two `70mm × 45mm` halves with `padding-top: 3mm` (so a 48 mm pitch), nine books per sheet. The Avery-like stocks are examples and **all values must be checked on the real sheet with the calibration page** [VERIFY].

### G.1 `pair-default` (default kind)

The built-in label templates are the folders of `config/labels/templates/`; their files are the reference.

`template.json`: kind `pair`, the unit of two stacked cells (`top.html`, `bottom.html`), the stocks it prints on (`a4-70x48`, `a4-70x24`), the variants by cell height (`full`; `compact` with files of its own) and the barcode size.

`top.html` (spine band): the copy's barcode, the short title, the pictogram, the head of the call number and, below it, the rest of the call number (fiction) or the classification label (non-fiction).

`bottom.html` (barcode label): the call number, the short title, the barcode and its number.

`style.css` (the renderer sizes each cell to the stock's `labelMm` and scopes these rules): the type, sizes and spacing of both labels, bold sans-serif, centred.

Rendered for the sample data of the reference printout this gives, per unit: top `2026-0267` / `La casa segreta` / pictogram `B` / `B 4` / `DEL`; bottom `B 4 DEL` / *La casa segreta* / barcode / `2026-0267` (the original printout used Code 39; new labels render Code 128, Q65). With the new barcode format the same book prints `2026-0267X` (`X` = Luhn digit, §3.7).

### G.2 Single-label templates

`barcode-default` (kind `barcode`, stocks `a4-70x48`, `l7159`, `a4-70x24`): `label.html` is the barcode label of G.1; on cells lower than 30 mm the variant `compact` uses `label-compact.html`.

`spine-default` (kind `spine`, stocks `a4-70x48`, `l7651`, `a4-70x24`): `label.html` is the spine band of G.1; on cells lower than 40 mm (e.g. `l7651`) the variant `compact` uses `label-compact.html`, a horizontal layout: a stripe in the classification's colour (hatched with `labels.monochrome`), the pictogram, the call number (head and rest) and the reading level in a circle.

### G.3 Print flow and reference files

Print flow: choose label kind (pair by default) and stock → choose items (selection, list, pending, acquisition date range) → choose the start position on the sheet (in units) → preview → print. The renderer lays units on the stock grid from the start position, draws outlines only on the calibration page, and marks items as printed on confirmation.

`docs/reference/labels/` holds `koha-etichette.html` (the page produced by the Koha plugin, with its CSS and per-label script, kept verbatim as the functional reference), `koha-etichette.png` (how it prints) and `README.md` (the measurements above and the mapping from the plugin's fields to BiblioViva's: `titolo` → `biblio.title_short`, `segnatura` → `item.call_number`, the *class or `S`* field → `classification.is_fiction` / `reading_class`, `codice` → `classification.code` with the hundreds rule replaced by parent inheritance, `categorieSaggistica` → `classification.nonfiction`).

## Appendix H — Overdue notice slip example (`slips/templates/overdue-by-class/`)

Reproduces the current "solleciti" printout (`docs/reference/overdues/README.md`): one block per group, in class order, each with a heading and a bordered three-column table. The files of `config/slips/templates/overdue-by-class/` are the reference.

`template.json`: name, description, kind `overdue`, page `A4 portrait` and its margin.

`slip.html` (rendered once per group; the sheet wrapper concatenates the blocks and adds `@page`): the heading "<class> - Solleciti biblioteca del <date>" and the table Nome / Titolo / Giorni ritardo, one row per overdue loan.

`style.css`: the block's font and spacing, the bordered table, the narrow last column.

The page size and margins come from `template.json` (`page`, `marginMm`); the sheet wrapper adds `@page` and, with `pageBreakPerGroup`, a page break after every block, so the template does not handle it.

Groups: students grouped by their current class (Phase 12) and labelled with its class label (`1 D`); teachers by their teachers group (`Docenti`); order: classes in the order of the years, then by name, then the teachers groups. Rows sorted by surname, then first name, then due date. `patron.full_name` is `first_name last_name`. The same data feeds `overdue-patron` (single block, no grouping) and the `overdue-loans` report.

## Appendix I — Circulation message catalogue (Q40)

Single source for the messages of the checkout and check-in desks. Every case has a service reason code, an i18n key (`circulation.msg.<code>`), the Italian text (English mirrored in `en/`), and the action offered as a button. Messages say what happened, why, and what to do; they never use jargon or ambiguous words. No sounds.

| Reason code | Where | Italian message | Action button |
|---|---|---|---|
| `no_patron_selected` | checkout, an item barcode is scanned before a card | "È stato letto il libro *{title}* senza una tessera. Leggere prima la tessera dell'utente; per una restituzione aprire Restituzioni." | "Vai a Restituzioni" (keeps the barcode) |
| `card_on_checkin` | check-in, a card is scanned | "Questa è una tessera ({name}), non un libro. Qui si registrano solo le restituzioni: per un prestito aprire Prestiti." | "Vai a Prestiti con questo utente" |
| `unknown_barcode` | both | "Codice {code} non trovato. Verificare la lettura dello scanner o digitare il codice: i codici dei libri hanno la forma {item}, i numeri delle tessere {card}." (examples of the configured formats, Phase 12) | "Riprova", "Cerca per titolo" |
| `bad_check_digit` | both | "Il codice {code} non è valido: il carattere di controllo non corrisponde. Rileggere il codice o digitarlo." | "Riprova" |
| `ambiguous_legacy` | both | "Il codice {code} corrisponde sia a una tessera ({name}) sia a un libro ({title}). Scegliere quale dei due." | "Tessera", "Libro" |
| `patron_anonymized` | checkout | "Questa tessera appartiene a un utente rimosso e non è più valida." | — |
| `loan_limit_reached` | checkout, **blocking** | "{name} ha già {n} libro/libri in prestito ({titles}); il limite è {max}. Per un nuovo prestito registrare prima una restituzione nella pagina Restituzioni." | "Vai a Restituzioni" (link only: no shortcut, no override; the return always requires scanning the returned item's barcode) |
| `item_on_loan_same_patron` | checkout | "*{title}* è già in prestito a {name} dal {date}. Rinnovare il prestito?" | "Rinnova", "Annulla" |
| `item_on_loan_other_patron` | checkout, blocking | "*{title}* risulta in prestito a {other} dal {date}. Registrare prima la restituzione nella pagina Restituzioni, poi tornare a Prestiti." | "Vai a Restituzioni" (the scanned barcode is carried over; the return is confirmed there, never done implicitly from the checkout page) |
| `item_not_for_loan` | checkout | "*{title}* è una copia da consultazione e non si presta." | "Annulla", "Forza (solo admin)" |
| `item_status_unavailable` | checkout | "*{title}* risulta {status} e non può essere prestato. Se il libro è tornato disponibile, aggiornarne lo stato dalla scheda." | "Apri scheda copia", "Forza (solo admin)" (never for a withdrawn copy) |
| `item_unclassified` | checkout, blocking (9.21) | "*{title}* non è ancora classificato e non si presta. Va classificato prima: genere o classe." | "Classifica" (with `catalog`) |
| `renewal_limit_reached` | renew | "*{title}* è già stato rinnovato {n} volte, il massimo. Deve essere restituito." | "Restituisci", "Forza (solo admin)" |
| `renewal_overdue_blocked` | renew | "*{title}* è in ritardo di {days} giorni e le regole non consentono il rinnovo." | "Restituisci", "Forza (solo admin)" |
| `renewal_hard_due_date` | renew | "*{title}* non si può rinnovare: la scadenza è già il {due}, l'ultimo giorno di prestito di quest'anno." | "Restituisci" |
| `duplicate_scan` | checkout session | "*{title}* è già nell'elenco di questo prestito." | — |
| `not_on_loan` | check-in | "*{title}* non risulta in prestito: nessuna restituzione da registrare. Se era prestato senza registrazione, il libro è comunque disponibile." | — |
| `already_returned_today` | check-in | "*{title}* è già stato restituito oggi alle {time}." | — |
| `returned_overdue` | check-in | "Restituito. *{title}* era in ritardo di {days} giorni (scadenza {due})." | "Segna danneggiato" |
| `was_lost_now_found` | check-in | "*{title}* era segnato come smarrito: ora torna disponibile." | "Conferma" |
| `patron_has_overdues` | checkout (info, not blocking unless `blockIfOverdue`) | "{name} ha {n} libro/libri in ritardo: {titles}." | "Stampa promemoria" |
| `calendar_closed_due_date` | checkout (info) | "La scadenza cade in un giorno di chiusura ed è stata spostata a {due}." | — |
| `hard_due_date_applied` | checkout (info) | "La scadenza è stata limitata al {due}, fine del prestito per quest'anno." | — |
| `session_idle_reset` | checkout | "Nessuna operazione da {n} minuti: il prestito di {name} è stato chiuso. Per continuare, leggere di nuovo la tessera." | — |
| `undo_done` | checkout | "Annullato: *{title}* non è più in prestito a {name}." | — |

Confirmations, which are not anomalies, use keys under `circulation.checkout.*` and `circulation.checkin.*` with their own codes in the page: `lent`, `renewed`, `returned`, `renewed_instead`, `marked_damaged`, and `checkin_pending` (a barcode carried over from the checkout desk waits in the field until the operator presses Enter).

Rules: the loan limit and the "item on loan to someone else" cases are hard blocks with no override, because pupils often forget the book they already have and the return must always be registered explicitly by scanning the book (Q13, Q40); placeholders come from the DTO, HTML-escaped; titles are shortened to 40 characters; every message has a Playwright assertion on its exact text; adding a case means adding a row here first.

*End of plan.*
